# Skills · L · part 16

800 entries, ld-performance-gap-analysis to lead-qualifier. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ld-performance-gap-analysis**: Diagnoses one performance problem before anything gets built, producing desired versus actual performance, a cause table that separates skill and knowledge from information, tools, incentives and process, non-training options and a recommendation. Use for "run ld-performance-gap-analysis", "performance gap analysis", "is it a training problem", "root cause of a performance problem", "training is not the answer", "job aid instead of a course", "why are people not doing it", "cause analysis", part of the AI for L&D and Corporate Training Pack by 90n. ([polar-bear-org/claude-skills](https://github.com/polar-bear-org/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/packs/ai-for-training-pack/skills/ld-performance-gap-analysis) · ★62)
- **ld-permissions**: Guide for Lightdash's CASL-based authorization system. Use when working with scopes, custom roles, abilities, permissions, ForbiddenError, authorization, or access control. Helps with adding new scopes, debugging permission issues, understanding the permission flow, and creating custom roles. ([lightdash/lightdash](https://github.com/lightdash/lightdash/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ld-permissions) · ★6.2k · 3 copies)
- **ld-priorities**: The household to-do list — every todo, task, or "add this to the list" the owner or a trusted household member gives you in chat ("create a todo: check who can come Oct 12", "add X to our to-do list", "remind me to renew the passports", "X is done", "what's next?", "call the list Weekend jobs", "no, the passport is more urgent"). Items live in this list and show on the wall card; the assistant owns the order and keeps the rules it learns. Not Apple Reminders: use that only when the owner names the Reminders app or their iPhone. Never on a schedule. ([plow-pbc/life-assistant-hermes-agent](https://github.com/plow-pbc/life-assistant-hermes-agent/tree/HEAD/ld-priorities) · ★4)

- **ld-setup**: First-run onboarding over chat. Meet the owner, learn their name, send them to install Plow Latch, collect their city and teams into /var/lib/hermes/ld/config.json as each lands, and show calendars from the background snapshot (never ask them to type one). Use in the owner's DM whose roster is just the two of you, on their message or on Plow setup's first-boot wakeup, which gets only the opener, while /var/lib/hermes/ld/config.json is missing any of family.owner.introduced, weather.location, sports.followed or calendar.sources, or has empty calendar.sources. Never use it in a group or in a DM… ([plow-pbc/life-assistant-hermes-agent](https://github.com/plow-pbc/life-assistant-hermes-agent/tree/HEAD/ld-setup) · ★4)

Use when the owner asks to set up or re-set-up their wall, when its config is missing or refused, or when the wall has never shown a card; never in a group or in a DM from anyone else. Onboarding is ld-setup's and finishes without a wall — an owner who never wants a screen in their kitchen never runs this. Do not use for unrelated calendar or life-assistant questions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/lda-class-separation-scoring))
- **lda-coherence-scoring-and-convergence-checking**: Use when training an LDA topic model over bag-of-words BGC (sub)Pfam tokenisations (e.g. iPRESTO PRESTO-TOP) and you must verify topic quality (u_mass coherence) and training convergence before using the motifs downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lda-coherence-scoring-and-convergence-checking))
- **lda-collapsed-gibbs-sampler-execution**: Use when a cisTopic object holding a binarized accessibility matrix (e.g. 5,335 PBMC cells × ~97k regions) is ready and a sweep of LDA topic models across a topic-number grid is required for subsequent model selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/lda-collapsed-gibbs-sampler-execution))
- **lda-convergence-monitoring-and-iteration**: Use when after configuring LDA hyperparameters (alpha, beta, number of topics, iteration budget) and loading a preprocessed bag-of-fragments corpus with neutral losses extracted and noise filtered, initiate LDA training and apply convergence monitoring to determine when topic-fragment probability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lda-convergence-monitoring-and-iteration) · 2 copies)
- **lda-effect-size-biomarker-ranking**: Use when a genus-level ASV abundance table split into predefined classes (e.g., hypervariable region combinations) must be screened for differentially abundant taxa and ranked as biomarkers by LDA effect size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/lda-effect-size-biomarker-ranking))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/lda-log-likelihood-comparison))
- **lda-model-fitting**: Use when you have a cells-by-markers dataframe (e.g., CyTOF Morphometry, T cell metabolic regulome, or Chromotyping data) with an a priori class-label vector (cell type, cell-cycle phase, time point) and want supervised dimensionality reduction onto LD axes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lda-result-database-persistence) · 2 copies)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/lda-topic-model-fitting))
